Skip to content
[img: SIE and PlayStation logo]

Director, Product Innovation
Location(s):
United States, San Mateo, CA
Job ID:
R-124961
Apply Now
Share
Why Sony Interactive Entertainment?
Sony Interactive Entertainment isn’t just the Best Place to Play — it’s also the Best Place to Work. Sony Interactive Entertainment (SIE) is the company behind the PlayStation brand. As a subsidiary of Sony Group Corporation, we’re part of a proud legacy of innovation and excellence. SIE is a dynamic technology company, delivering cutting-edge hardware and network services to more than 100 million people and an entertainment leader, home to some of the most beloved and recognizable intellectual properties (IP) in the world. Our role at SIE is to create and nurture the experiences under the PlayStation brand, a name synonymous with entertainment excellence and creativity.
The Marketing Director, Product Innovation is a product management leader that owns the vision, strategy, and roadmap for PlayStation's global marketing technology products, building platforms that empower thousands of marketers and reach hundreds of millions of players worldwide. You will lead forward-looking, AI-powered marketing capabilities, creating scalable solutions that enable marketers worldwide to deliver better business outcomes. This role sits at the intersection of marketing operations, data science, analytics, and technology -- increasing efficiency, accelerating execution, and amplifying creativity.
We are seeking an experienced Product Manager who is a development partner with Engineering, Data Science and Marketing executives to build marketing products that operate at a global scale.
Key Responsibilities:
Product Management
Partner with Engineering leaders to define platform architecture, prioritize investments, balance technical debt against innovation, and deliver scalable products that transform global marketing capabilities.
Define Marketing Technology product vision informed by customer research, market trends, and internal user insights.
Conduct product discovery with Marketing teams globally.
Develop a prioritized roadmap based on customer value, technical feasibility, business impact, and other considerations.
Launch new platform capabilities and measure adoption, engagement, and business outcomes.
Drive continuous experimentation using AI-enabled workflows.
Assess opportunities for assistive AI and agentic marketing use cases, manage pilot execution, and ensure scalability.
Marketing Innovation
Bridge business requirements from Marketing to Engineering to create technology solutions.
Own the process, the outcomes, and the products.
Thought leader and strategic partner to Media, Creative, Brand Marketing, Product Marketing and Business Analytics teams.
Demonstrate a solutions-oriented mindset, troubleshooting challenges and adapting plans to meet evolving business and market needs.
Balance global alignment with appropriate local and product-level flexibility, reinforcing a one SIE mindset while respecting market and portfolio needs.
Strategy & Transformation
Lead high-priority initiatives, aligning focus, managing execution, driving business outcomes, and measuring success.
Conduct market and feasibility assessments of new tools, trends, and advancements in marketing technology and data enablement.
Shape short- and long-term roadmaps, driving long-term scalability while balancing day-to-day improvements.
Establish alignment across SIE teams that own or influence the marketing technology stack, driving a single, cohesive product vision and roadmap.
Partner with marketing leaders to redesign marketing workflows, processes, decision rights, and operating models.
Define measures of success and post-launch KPI tracking.
Skills and Qualifications:
Bachelor’s degree or equivalent in software engineering, computer science, engineering management or a related field. Master’s degree or equivalent in business, marketing, or related field preferred.
12+ years of product management experience, within marketing solutions or a high-growth technology environment.
Built and launched enterprise SaaS products or internal platforms.
Owned multi-year product roadmaps.
Led cross-functional product teams across Engineering, Design, Data Science, and Business.
Experience with AI-powered products, ML platforms, or automation.
Strong understanding of experimentation, analytics, APIs, and platform thinking.
Experience scaling products used globally.
Experience in navigating change and driving global teams through transformation.
Experience influencing senior leadership and leading cross-functional teams across multiple regions.
At SIE, we consider several factors when setting each role’s base pay range, including the competitive benchmarking data for the market and geographic location.
Please note that the base pay range may vary in line with our hybrid working policy and individual base pay will be determined based on job-related factors which may include knowledge, skills, experience, and location.
In addition, this role is eligible for SIE’s top-tier benefits package that includes medical, dental, vision, matching 401(k), paid time off, wellness program and coveted employee discounts for Sony products. This role also may be eligible for a bonus package. Click here to learn more.
The estimated base pay range for this role is listed below.
$234,100—$351,100 USD
Please note, Sony Interactive Entertainment conducts background checks at the offer stage for all new employees (which may include criminal background checks for some roles) and will need to process personal information to support these checks.
Please refer to our Candidate Privacy Notice (where applicable) for more information about what personal information we collect, how we use it, who 
```

Director, Product Management - Data Science
Location(s):
United States, San Mateo, CA
Job ID:

Why Sony Interactive Entertainment?
Sony Interactive Entertainment isn’t just the Best Place to Play — it’s also the Best Place to Work. Sony Interactive Entertainment (SIE) is the company behind the PlayStation brand. As a subsidiary of Sony Group Corporation, we’re part of a proud legacy of innovation and excellence. SIE is a dynamic technology company, delivering cutting-edge hardware and network services to more than 100 million people and an entertainment leader, home to some of the most beloved and recognizable intellectual properties (IP) in the world. Our role at SIE is to create and nurture the experiences under the PlayStation brand, a name synonymous with entertainment excellence and creativity.
Director, Product Management - Data Science
San Mateo, CA
At Sony Interactive Entertainment, PlayStation creates unforgettable gaming experiences for millions of players around the world. As a global leader in interactive entertainment, we operate at massive scale across a complex digital ecosystem spanning hardware, software, services, commerce, marketing, and player engagement.
About the Role
We are seeking a Director of Data Science Product Management to lead the strategy, prioritization, and execution of data science to drive better decision-making and player experiences across PlayStation.
This role is responsible for portfolio of products across the data science lifecycle - identifying high-value analytical opportunities, shaping predictive and decision products, and ensuring they deliver measurable business outcomes through adoption, monitoring and continuous improvement. The role will lead a team of Data Science Product Managers responsible for applying data science to solve business problems, including capabilities across customer lifetime value, player lifecycle intelligence, forecasting, simulations, audience intelligence and decision automation.
The Director will help ensure that SIE’s data science investments are focused on the highest-impact opportunities, built with strong product discipline, and adopted in the workflows where they can create measurable business value.
Key Responsibilities
Translate business priorities into structured problem statements, investment cases, product strategies, and measurable outcomes, ensuring data science work is managed as a scalable product portfolio rather than one-off analytical requests.
Drive product strategy and lifecycle ownership for player value and decision intelligence capabilities, such as CLV, engagement and forecasting, ensuring high quality products are advanced from discovery through productionization, adoption, monitoring, iteration, and long-term value realization.
Establish clear criteria for when analytical work should remain exploratory, become a recurring decision process, or graduate into a production-grade product.
Define success metrics and quality standards for data science products, including business impact, adoption, reliability, interpretability, model quality, operational sustainability, and decision quality.
Partner with Data Science and Analytics leaders to ensure rigor across experimentation, causal inference, forecasting, incrementality measurement, model evaluation, and value analysis, while promoting these approaches as core decision-making tools.
Influence senior stakeholders across functions to align strategy, clarify priorities, manage tradeoffs, and drive adoption of data science products.
Guide Product Managers across the product lifecycle while directly leading or sponsoring the organization's highest-impact and most ambiguous product initiatives.
Create a strong Data Science product culture centered on hypothesis-driven discovery, evidence-based prioritization, measurable business impact and close partnership with cross-functional partners.
Qualifications
Bachelor’s degree in Business, Data Science, Computer Science, Statistics, Economics, Engineering, or a related field.
15+ years of relevant experience, including 8+ years in digital product management, data product management, AI/ML product management, analytics product management, or related roles.
6+ years of people management experience, including experience leading Product Managers or senior product-oriented analytics leaders.
Proven experience leading complex, cross-functional portfolios involving Data Science, ML Engineering, Analytics, Data Engineering, and business stakeholders.
Strong understanding of data science and machine learning workflows, including experimentation, predictive modeling, forecasting, segmentation, recommender systems, lifecycle modeling, productionization, and monitoring.
Experience with products or capabilities related to CLV/LTV, personalization, churn, propensity modeling, forecasting, audience targeting, marketing optimization, commerce, or digital engagement.
Familiarity with modern data and ML ecosystems such as SQL, Python, Snowflake, Spark, feature stores, MLOps tools, model monitoring, APIs, and analytics platforms.
Demonstrated ability to translate ambiguous business needs into product strategies, roadmaps, priorities, and measurable outcomes.
Strong executive communication skills, including the ability to translate technical concepts into business impact.
Experience leading, mentoring, and developing high-performing product teams.
Preferred Skills
Experience in gaming, digital commerce, subscription, marketplace, streaming, or consumer technology businesses.
Deep experience with customer intelligence, player lifecycle analytics, personalization, experimentation, audience intelligence, or growth optimization.
Experience defining success metrics and 
```

Why Sony Interactive Entertainment?
Sony Interactive Entertainment isn’t just the Best Place to Play — it’s also the Best Place to Work. Sony Interactive Entertainment (SIE) is the company behind the PlayStation brand. As a subsidiary of Sony Group Corporation, we’re part of a proud legacy of innovation and excellence. SIE is a dynamic technology company, delivering cutting-edge hardware and network services to more than 100 million people and an entertainment leader, home to some of the most beloved and recognizable intellectual properties (IP) in the world. Our role at SIE is to create and nurture the experiences under the PlayStation brand, a name synonymous with entertainment excellence and creativity.
Director, Program Management
Portfolio & Program Management Office (PPMO)
About the Role
Sony Interactive Entertainment is seeking a Director, Program Management to lead the delivery, governance, and strategic execution of critical e-commerce, payments, and monetization initiatives across the PlayStation platform.
This role is responsible for leading a portfolio of large-scale, cross-functional programs that span Product, Engineering, Finance, Tax, Legal, Operations, Customer Experience, and Commercial organizations. The Director will drive alignment across senior leaders, influence strategic investment decisions, and ensure complex initiatives deliver measurable customer and business outcomes.
The ideal candidate combines deep program leadership expertise with strong domain knowledge in digital commerce, payments, subscription businesses, marketplaces, checkout experiences, fraud prevention, taxation, and financial operations. They must be capable of operating at both strategic and execution levels, translating business objectives into scalable delivery plans while ensuring disciplined program execution.
What You'll Do
Strategic Portfolio Leadership
Lead a portfolio of complex, high-visibility initiatives supporting PlayStation's commerce and monetization ecosystem.
Partner with executive leaders to define strategic objectives, roadmaps, priorities, success metrics, and investment plans.
Drive alignment across global organizations and mission groups with competing priorities and dependencies.
Establish operating models and governance frameworks that enable informed decision-making, accountability, and effective risk management.
Serve as a strategic partner to senior leadership on portfolio health, execution strategy, organizational readiness, capacity planning, and investment decisions.
Commerce & Payments Transformation
Partner with Product Management and Engineering leaders to evaluate business opportunities and develop scalable execution strategies.
Ensure business, operational, regulatory, compliance, and customer experience requirements are incorporated into planning and delivery.
Drive initiatives that improve conversion, payment success rates, operational efficiency, platform scalability, compliance readiness, and customer satisfaction.
Contribute to the evolution of player experiences in discovering and transacting in their journeys.
Portfolio Execution & Benefits Realization
Establish and maintain portfolio-level planning, dependency management, reporting, forecasting, and executive communications.
Identify delivery risks, resource constraints, and execution challenges early and drive mitigation plans.
Oversee complex cross-organizational dependencies across multiple teams, business units, and external partners.
Facilitate executive reviews, planning cadences, governance forums, and portfolio decision-making processes.
Define and track outcome-based KPIs, benefits realization plans, and success metrics that demonstrate measurable business value.
Organizational Leadership
Build and lead a program management organization focused on execution excellence, accountability, and continuous improvement.
Support the professional development and growth of program managers and senior program leaders.
Build strong partnerships with senior leaders across Product, Engineering, Finance, Legal, Tax, Operations, and Commercial organizations.
Drive a culture of accountability, clarity, transparency, operational excellence, and customer focus.
Influence execution across organizations without direct authority while maintaining strong stakeholder alignment.
Qualifications
Required
Significant experience leading large-scale technology programs, portfolios, or product delivery organizations.
Experience leading complex cross-functional programs involving multiple engineering, product, and business organizations.
Demonstrated success operating within large, matrixed global organizations.
Strong executive communication, stakeholder management, and organizational influence skills.
Experience establishing governance models, portfolio management processes, operating rhythms, and executive reporting frameworks.
Strong analytical and problem-solving abilities with a track record of translating ambiguity into actionable plans.
Experience leading mission-critical platforms, large-scale transactional systems, digital commerce ecosystems, or marketplace businesses serving millions of customers.
Commerce & Payments Experience
Deep experience in one or more of the following areas:
E-commerce platforms
Digital payments
Payment processing and orchestration
Checkout experiences
Subscription businesses
Digital marketplaces
Billing systems
Fraud prevention and payments risk management
Financial operations platforms
Tax and regulatory compliance
Additional requirements:
Demonstrated expertise in end-to-end digital commerce ecosystems, including revenue flows, customer transactions
```

Engine and Tools Programming Intern – Undergraduate
Location(s):
United States, Burbank, CA
Job ID:
R-125841
Apply Now
Share
Job ID: R-125841
Role Summary:
Join the engineering team at Insomniac Games and gain hands-on experience developing the game engine technology that supports the creation of our games.
As an Engine Programmer Intern, you'll work alongside experienced programmers to design, develop, and improve the runtime and tools components of Insomniac’s engine technology. You'll primarily use C++ to build efficient systems, improve performance, and create tools that help content creators develop game assets.
Depending on your interests and expertise, you'll have opportunities to explore tools development, animation, low-level engine architecture, or rendering. This internship offers practical experience with real-time game engine development, technical problem-solving, and collaboration with production teams while learning how engine technology supports game development.
What You’ll Be Doing:
Design and develop game engine technology with guidance from team leads, focusing on real-time performance and usability.
Build efficient low-level systems that support higher-level pipelines and game development workflows.
Design and implement tools that help content creators develop and manage game assets.
Maintain and improve existing engine systems to enhance functionality, performance, and reliability.
Explore the capabilities and limitations of specific game platform hardware to better understand how it affects engine development.
Collaborate with production teams to identify technical challenges, troubleshoot issues, and develop practical solutions.
Investigate and respond to bug reports, feature requests, and questions related to engine technology.
Document technical implementation details to support collaboration and ongoing development.
Explore technological developments in the game industry and learn how new approaches can support engine development.
What We’re Looking For:
Currently pursuing a degree in Computer Science, Computer Graphics, or a related field.
Proficiency in programming languages such as C++.
Experience with game engines or game development patterns.
Strong problem-solving and debugging skills, with the ability to investigate and resolve technical issues.
Strong communication and collaboration skills, with the ability to work effectively with artists and programmers.
Interest in learning about emerging game development technologies and staying informed about industry trends.
Eligibility:
Students with expected graduation between May 2026 and December 2029 from an accredited university. Undergraduate
Able to work during one of the following periods: May 17th - August 6th or June 14th - September 3rd. Relocation assistance is available for eligible candidates.
Must be available for the entire 12-week internship period.
At this time, SIE will only consider applicants who are authorized to work for any employer in the United States and who will not require work visa sponsorship from SIE now or in the future to maintain their authorization to work in the United States.
Our intern positions offer a fixed hourly pay rate. Please note that the pay rate is exclusive to the internship period and may not reflect what pay may look like following the completion of an internship
At SIE, we consider several factors when setting each role’s base pay range, including the competitive benchmarking data for the market and geographic location.
Please note that the base pay range may vary in line with our hybrid working policy and individual base pay will be determined based on job-related factors which may include knowledge, skills, experience, and location.
Intern Pay Range
$45.75—$45.75 USD
Please note, Sony Interactive Entertainment conducts background checks at the offer stage for all new employees (which may include criminal background checks for some roles) and will need to process personal information to support these checks.
Please refer to our Candidate Privacy Notice for more information about what personal information we collect, how we use it, who we share it with, and your data protection rights.

Engineering Manager – Enterprise Data Engineering
Location(s):
Ireland, Dublin
Job ID:
R-125802
Apply Now
Share
Why Sony Interactive Entertainment?
Sony Interactive Entertainment isn’t just the Best Place to Play — it’s also the Best Place to Work. Sony Interactive Entertainment (SIE) is the company behind the PlayStation brand. As a subsidiary of Sony Group Corporation, we’re part of a proud legacy of innovation and excellence. SIE is a dynamic technology company, delivering cutting-edge hardware and network services to more than 100 million people and an entertainment leader, home to some of the most beloved and recognizable intellectual properties (IP) in the world. Our role at SIE is to create and nurture the experiences under the PlayStation brand, a name synonymous with entertainment excellence and creativity.
Engineering Manager – Enterprise Data Engineering
We are looking for an experienced Engineering Manager to lead a team of Enterprise Data Engineers delivering trusted, scalable and reusable data capabilities across Sony Interactive Entertainment (SIE). The role combines people leadership, technical direction, delivery ownership and operational accountability for enterprise data products, pipelines and integrations.
Working with global engineering, architecture, platform, security and business teams, you will enable enterprise priorities spanning Finance, Supply Chain, IT Operations, People & Places and Sales & Operations. You will balance transformation delivery with the reliability, maintainability and continuous improvement of existing data services.
Responsibilities
Lead, coach and grow the engineering team
· Manage performance, development, recruitment and succession; create clear goals, regular feedback and meaningful career plans.
· Build a culture of ownership, inclusion, engineering discipline, collaboration and continuous learning.
· Provide hands-on technical guidance when needed while delegating decisions and developing senior engineering capability.
Own delivery, capacity and execution
· Translate enterprise roadmaps into executable plans, milestones, dependencies and measurable outcomes; balance project commitments with run-the-business obligations.
· Proactively forecast capacity and skills needs, allocate resources, manage competing priorities and identify delivery trade-offs.
· Maintain clear visibility of progress, blockers and risks; escalate early with options and recommendations, and communicate confidently to leadership and stakeholders.
· Drive predictable delivery through appropriate agile practices, estimation, design reviews, testing, release management and continuous improvement.
Provide technical leadership for enterprise data engineering
· Guide architecture and implementation of secure, scalable batch and streaming ingestion, ETL/ELT pipelines, transformations and enterprise integrations.
· Champion maintainable engineering patterns across Snowflake, Databricks, Python/PySpark, SQL, dbt and relevant orchestration and integration technologies.
· Ensure data models and domain-owned data products are reusable, discoverable, documented and aligned with enterprise standards.
· Embed source control, peer review, automated testing, CI/CD, infrastructure-as-code where appropriate, and sound software engineering practices.
Own operational quality and production readiness
· Be accountable for service reliability, observability, data freshness, data quality, lineage, access controls and operational support for team-owned pipelines and products.
· Establish effective monitoring, alerting, incident response, root-cause analysis, recovery procedures and reduction of recurring support effort.
· Partner with platform, architecture, security and governance teams on production readiness, privacy, compliance, cost and performance optimisation.
Partner across the enterprise
· Work with domain and transformation leaders to understand business outcomes, shape requirements and prioritise sustainable data engineering solutions.
· Coordinate dependencies with data platform, analytics, AI/ML, application and infrastructure teams to enable end-to-end delivery.
· Represent the team’s architecture, products, delivery status, risks and impact in technical and executive forums.
Skills and Qualifications
· 3+ years of people management or engineering leadership experience, including performance management, coaching and leading delivery teams.
· 8+ years of relevant software, data platform or data engineering experience, with demonstrated ownership of production-grade enterprise data solutions.
· Strong understanding of data architecture, ETL/ELT, data warehousing/lakehouse patterns, dimensional modelling, data contracts and reusable data products.
· Practical technical experience with SQL and Python; strong familiarity with Snowflake, Databricks and PySpark, and experience with dbt or comparable transformation frameworks.
· Experience with orchestration, API/file-based integrations, cloud services (preferably AWS), Git-based development, automated testing and CI/CD.
· Experience operating business-critical data pipelines, including monitoring, incident management, data quality, security and performance optimisation.
· Ability to plan team capacity, manage a portfolio of priorities, resolve cross-team dependencies and provide transparent, proactive delivery reporting.
· Excellent stakeholder management and communication skills across engineering, business and senior leadership; undergraduate degree or equivalent experience.
Desirable Experience
· Enterprise Finance or Supply Chain data, including SAP and planning-system integrations (such as Anaplan or Adaptive).
· Data governance, metadata/lineage, master data, privacy classification and regu

Environment Art Intern- Undergraduate
Location(s):
United States, Burbank, CA
Job ID:
R-125850
Apply Now
Share
Job ID: R-125850
Role Summary:
Join the Art Department at Insomniac Games and gain hands-on experience creating environments and assets that help bring game worlds to life.
As an Environment Art Intern, you'll collaborate with experienced artists to model, texture, surface, and place assets within game environments. You'll contribute to creating natural and architectural elements while learning how environment art supports the visual design of a game.
Throughout the internship, you'll receive mentorship from senior members of the Environment art team, explore new artistic techniques and tools, and gain practical experience working within a game development studio.
What You’ll Be Doing:
Model natural and architectural geometry using concept art and photo reference, with a focus on creating props and supporting level artists.
Create UV layouts, generate normal maps, and develop textures and materials for environment objects.
Contribute to modeling, texturing, surfacing, and placing component assets to help build game worlds.
Learn and apply new artistic techniques and tools to maintain a consistent visual style under the direction of the Art Director.
Collaborate with the Environment art team to develop and refine assets while gaining hands-on experience with game production workflows.
What We’re Looking For:
Currently enrolled at a college or university studying a related Arts field. No prior professional experience or training required.
A creative, self-motivated, and responsible approach to learning and completing assigned work.
Passion for video games and art, with an interest in environment modeling, texturing, and game development.
Willingness to learn new tools and techniques and collaborate with experienced artists.
Python skills and previous experience with Maya are preferred.
Eligibility:
Students with expected graduation between May 2026 and December 2029 from an accredited university. Undergraduate
Able to work during one of the following periods: May 17th - August 6th or June 14th - September 3rd. Relocation assistance is available for eligible candidates.
Must be available for the entire 12-week internship period.
At this time, SIE will only consider applicants who are authorized to work for any employer in the United States and who will not require work visa sponsorship from SIE now or in the future to maintain their authorization to work in the United States.
Our intern positions offer a fixed hourly pay rate. Please note that the pay rate is exclusive to the internship period and may not reflect what pay may look like following the completion of an internship.
At SIE, we consider several factors when setting each role’s base pay range, including the competitive benchmarking data for the market and geographic location.
Please note that the base pay range may vary in line with our hybrid working policy and individual base pay will be determined based on job-related factors which may include knowledge, skills, experience, and location.
Intern Pay Range
$36.50—$36.50 USD
Please note, Sony Interactive Entertainment conducts background checks at the offer stage for all new employees (which may include criminal background checks for some roles) and will need to process personal information to support these checks.
Please refer to our Candidate Privacy Notice for more information about what personal information we collect, how we use it, who we share it with, and your data protection rights.

Flow Production Tracking Administrator (CONTRACT)
Location(s):
United States, Remote
Job ID:
R-124826
Apply Now
Share
Flow Production Tracking Administrator (6-month CONTRACT) This position is for temporary engagement through our staffing partner, Yoh Enterprise Solutions. Candidates must be authorized to work in the United States for this position.
Insomniac Games is looking for a Flow Production Tracking Administrator. In this role, the Flow Administrator is responsible for stabilizing and maintaining our Flow Production Tracking (formerly ShotGrid) environment, with an initial focus on the Flow/Jira Bridge that underpins our daily production workflow. Flow is the system of record for production tracking across 300+ users and thousands of tasks per month. The initial scope of the engagement is 6 months, prioritizing bridge stability before expanding to broader Flow improvements.
ESSENTIAL FUNCTIONS AND RESPONSIBILITIES
Stands up a studio-owned instance of the Flow/Jira Bridge so that bridge configuration, deployment, and recovery can be managed internally without dependency on Sony Interactive Entertainment HQ.
Stabilizes and maintains the Flow/Jira Bridge as the first priority of the engagement, ensuring data integrity between Flow and Jira.
Re-architects the Flow automation and event listener layer for reliability and security, including migration off the current virtual machine to infrastructure that is not prone to unscheduled restarts.
Troubleshoots Flow and Jira Bridge issues for internal developers and external development partners; serves as the primary point of escalation when the system is not behaving.
Audits and documents the current Flow and Jira Bridge configuration, integration architecture, and recovery procedures.
Fixes broken or degraded areas of the Flow pipeline as scoped with pipeline engineering, beginning with the Character workflow.
Sets up and improves Flow pipelines for upcoming work, including VFX, and contributes ongoing improvements to the Cinematics pipeline.
Administers Flow Production Tracking day-to-day: schema, permissions, page layouts, custom fields, entity relationships, and project setup.
Configures and supports vendor (external partner) permissions and workflows, ensuring information access is appropriate to each partnership.
Designs and builds Flow dashboards, reports, and custom pages to meet production and leadership reporting needs.
Other duties may be assigned.
EXPERIENCE AND EDUCATION
Five plus years of professional experience administering Flow Production Tracking, ShotGrid, or Shotgun in a games, VFX, or animation production environment, or an equivalent amount of education and experience.
Demonstrated experience operating in a multi-vendor co-development environment.
Shipped at least one AAA title with Flow/ShotGrid as the production system of record preferred.
TECHNICAL SKILLS
Hands-on experience with the sg-jira-bridge or a comparable Flow/Jira integration, including reading, modifying, and deploying the Python service independently.
Working knowledge of the Flow Python API and Toolkit (tk-core, pipeline configurations, YAML configuration).
Experience deploying and operating the Flow event listener and automation framework as a reliable service rather than as a fragile script on a virtual machine.
Proficiency with Jira administration, including workflows, custom fields, issue types, and the Jira REST API.
Comfort designing permissions and information access for mixed internal and external (vendor) user populations.
Familiarity with Perforce, Maya, and standard DCC pipelines.
Experience with self-hosted services, containerization, or CI/CD for pipeline tooling is a plus.
Experience with RV, Deadline, or other render/review tool integrations is a plus.
SQL and reporting or BI tool experience is a plus.
OTHER SKILLS
Strong written communication; able to produce documentation that others can use to operate and recover the system.
Ability to triage between time-sensitive production issues and longer-term platform work without losing momentum on either.
High degree of autonomy and accountability; proactively identifies and resolves production issues without requiring close oversight.
Collaborative and solutions-oriented mindset with a track record of building strong working relationships with internal teams and external partners.
Ability to work effectively across time zones with overseas external development partners.
At SIE, working with our partners, we consider several factors when setting each role’s base pay range, including the competitive benchmarking data for the market and geographic location.
Please note that the individual base pay range may vary based on job-related factors which may include knowledge, skills, experience, and location.
In addition, this role is eligible for benefit offerings that include medical, dental, and vision. Click here to learn more.
The estimated base pay range for this role is listed below, this is an hourly rate.
$84—$84 USD
Please note, Sony Interactive Entertainment conducts background checks at the offer stage for all new employees (which may include criminal background checks for some roles) and will need to process personal information to support these checks.
Please refer to our Candidate Privacy Notice for more information about what personal information we collect, how we use it, who we share it with, and your data protection rights.

Frontend Software Engineering Intern - Undergraduate
Location(s):
United States, Los Angeles, CA; United States, San Mateo, CA
Job ID:
R-125838
Apply Now
Share
Role Summary:
Join the Audience Platform team at Sony Interactive Entertainment (SIE) and gain hands-on experience developing internal tools that support promotions, advertising, and marketing campaigns across PlayStation.
Our team builds applications that help internal business users create and manage audience segments. We turn complex audience data and workflows into accessible, user-friendly interfaces for segment creation, discovery, comparison, and insights.
As a Frontend Software Engineering Intern, you'll work alongside experienced engineers to develop and improve features in a production React application using JavaScript and TypeScript. You'll take ownership of a defined feature or improvement, from understanding user needs through implementation, testing, and code review. This internship offers practical experience with frontend development, API integration, and collaborative engineering while learning how internal tools support business decisions.
What You’ll Be Doing:
Build and improve React components for audience workflows, including segment lists, forms, filters, details, and insights.
Translate product requirements and API data into clear, accessible user interfaces, including loading, empty, error, and permission-aware states.
Explore how application routes, components, data, and state work together to identify and implement focused improvements within a production codebase.
Write and update tests or component examples, verify functionality in the browser, and explain what your testing covers.
Collaborate with engineers, product partners, and reviewers to clarify requirements, discuss technical tradeoffs, and improve solutions through feedback.
Gain practical experience with TypeScript contracts, shared UI components, JSON, API wrappers, and a backend-for-frontend to understand how frontend applications interact with backend services.
Learn and apply development practices using Git, pull requests, code review, and testing tools such as Storybook, mock, unit, and browser testing workflows.
Explore how internal audience tools support marketing and analytics decisions across PlayStation, including how Sony uses LLMs daily and the latest AI technology.
What We’re Looking For:
Currently pursuing an undergraduate degree in Computer Science, Computer Engineering, or a related field.
Working knowledge of HTML, CSS, and JavaScript, with the ability to build and explain a small interactive web page or application.
Basic familiarity with React components, props, and state through coursework or personal projects, with a willingness to learn TypeScript in a production codebase.
Comfort reading JSON and making simple API requests, along with introductory experience using Git or another version control system.
Curiosity about how data, permissions, and shared components affect user interfaces, with a willingness to ask questions when requirements are unclear.
Clear communication, collaborative problem-solving skills, and openness to feedback and code review.
Java experience and MVC design pattern familiarity are a plus.
Interest in testing, accessibility, and clear user experiences is beneficial, with related coursework or project experience considered a plus.
Eligibility
Students with expected graduation between May 2026 and December 2029 from an accredited university. Undergraduate
Able to work during one of the following periods: May 17th - August 6th or June 14th - September 3rd. Relocation assistance is available for eligible candidates.
Must be available for the entire 12-week internship period.
At this time, SIE will only consider applicants who are authorized to work for any employer in the United States and who will not require work visa sponsorship from SIE now or in the future to maintain their authorization to work in the United States.
Our intern positions offer a fixed hourly pay rate. Please note that the pay rate is exclusive to the internship period and may not reflect what pay may look like following the completion of an internship.
At SIE, we consider several factors when setting each role’s base pay range, including the competitive benchmarking data for the market and geographic location.
Please note that the base pay range may vary in line with our hybrid working policy and individual base pay will be determined based on job-related factors which may include knowledge, skills, experience, and location.
Intern Pay Range
$45.75—$50 USD
Please note, Sony Interactive Entertainment conducts background checks at the offer stage for all new employees (which may include criminal background checks for some roles) and will need to process personal information to support these checks.
Please refer to our Candidate Privacy Notice for more information about what personal information we collect, how we use it, who we share it with, and your data protection rights.

Full Stack Software Engineer II (Contract Role)
Location(s):
United States, Madison, WI

Apply Now
Share
Why Sony Interactive Entertainment?
Sony Interactive Entertainment isn’t just the Best Place to Play — it’s also the Best Place to Work. Sony Interactive Entertainment (SIE) is the company behind the PlayStation brand. As a subsidiary of Sony Group Corporation, we’re part of a proud legacy of innovation and excellence. SIE is a dynamic technology company, delivering cutting-edge hardware and network services to more than 100 million people and an entertainment leader, home to some of the most beloved and recognizable intellectual properties (IP) in the world. Our role at SIE is to create and nurture the experiences under the PlayStation brand, a name synonymous with entertainment excellence and creativity.
Full Stack Software Engineer II (Contract Role)
Madison, WI
Playstation is looking for a Software Engineer to build our next-generation technologies and applications in the growing Madison, WI campus. We apply modern web technologies and methodologies to build an outstanding commerce platform and a customer experience filled with delight. The ideal candidate will come from an object-oriented development background, and be passionate about front end web technologies. Are you ready for a rare opportunity to deliver high-quality, thoroughly tested code at one of the world’s most recognizable brands? We’re doing really cool things with web technologies like React, React Native, and more to keep PlayStation the #1 gaming platform in the world.
Yes! There is a Sony Playstation office in Madison, WI! We are located in the heart of downtown right near the Capitol Square with an outstanding view of the east side and many great restaurants and social opportunities right outside the door.
This is a hybrid role. We offer a flexible work-from-home policy. In-person meetings may sometimes be required. Otherwise, you can work in the office as much or as little as you prefer. We also host many social and team bonding events in our Madison campus.
The team
This team focuses on building client application experiences for PlayStation consoles, websites, mobile applications and beyond. These features serve millions of users around the world. If you strive for outstanding UX, improving performance for a fast experience, and the blend of games and technology, please apply.
Responsibilities:
Develop, test and document business critical features and applications
Work with Product Owners and Product Managers to understand feature requirements, capture technical requirements and craft solutions for components and applications for the PlayStation ecosystem
Passionate about quality and strives to improve processes, proposes standard methodologies and drives their usage within the teams
Developing JavaScript-based applications for PlayStation gaming consoles, web, mobile and future flagship Sony devices and services
Building digital commerce solutions for PlayStation games, media content, subscription services and more
Write robust unit and component tests to ensure reliability and prevent regressions.
Work quickly and optimally in a fast-paced ever-evolving environment
Work independently and collaborate with internal and external engineers
Work with other team members by reviewing and giving feedback on pull requests and design plans
Define and refine technical approaches that support performance, accessibility, and scalability.
Exercise your feature building capabilities and collaborate within your team to optimize existing solutions as well
Deploying platform experiences through CI/CD pipelines
Monitoring software releases, analyze telemetry, and adapt to production data
Continuously learn, experiment, and bring forward innovative ideas — participate in Hackathons and Café Days to help shape the future of PlayStation experiences.
Required Qualifications:
Bachelor's degree or equivalent, or 3+ years of relevant work experience instead of a degree
2-3 years of building product experiences with proficiencies in UI technologies
A passion for robust sustainable and high-quality code
Excellent communication and collaboration skills
Strong technical, analytical, and problem-solving skills
Capable of producing detailed design and development documentation
Preferred Qualifications:
Using HTML, CSS, the JavaScript ecosystem and familiarity with popular frameworks like React, Angular, Jest, and TypeScript
Familiarity with Java and Spring Boot is a bonus
Familiarity with GraphQL, REST APIs, and containerization tools such as Docker.
Understanding of build pipelines and CI/CD standards, especially across multiple platforms.
Experience working with performance optimization, accessibility, and responsive design principles.
Curiosity about AI-assisted development tools or automation that improves developer efficiency
At SIE, working with our partners, we consider several factors when setting each role’s base pay range, including the competitive benchmarking data for the market and geographic location.
Please note that the individual base pay range may vary based on job-related factors which may include knowledge, skills, experience, and location.
In addition, this role is eligible for benefit offerings that include medical, dental, and vision. Click here to learn more.
The estimated base pay range for this role is listed below, this is an hourly rate.
$72.13—$108.20 USD
Please note, Sony Interactive Entertainment conducts background checks at the offer stage for all new employees (which may include criminal background checks for some roles) and will need to process personal information to support these checks.

200284

Share
「グランツーリスモ」制作メンバー募集
グランツーリスモを作り続けて25年が経ちました。
作り続けられた一番の理由は、プレイヤーがいてくれたことです。
それは必死に作り続けた作品が、きちんと響いてくれた証拠だと思っています。
未来のアイデアを、アートとテクノロジーによりできる限り実現してきたこと、それは私たちの誇りです。
しかし、プレイヤーに最高の体験を届けるにはまだ足りないといつも思うのです…
「アートとテクノロジーで未来をつくりたい」
尽きることのない創作意欲をもてあましているあなたへ。
情熱、技術、アイデアお待ちしております。
株式会社ポリフォニー・デジタルは、株式会社ソニー・インタラクティブエンタテインメント（SIE）の100%出資によるソフトウェア開発会社です。
全世界累計実売本数が1億本*を突破したドライビングシミュレーター「グランツーリスモ」シリーズを中心に、家庭用ゲームソフトウェアの企画・制作・サービス運営を行っています。
*2025年6月時点
https://www.polyphony.co.jp/
----
Now hiring staff for the production of "Gran Turismo"
We have continued to produce the Gran Turismo series for over 25 years now. The biggest reason we were able to continue this for so long, is the existence of our player base, and we believe it is proof that the hard work we poured our lives into have truly resonated with them. We are proud to have brought ideas of the future into reality over the years through art and technology.
But we still think there is much more to be done to provide the best experience for our players. For those of you who have that unending urge to create, to 'create the future through art and technology', we await your passion, skill, and ideas at Polyphony Digital.
Polyphony Digital is a software production company that is a 100% subsidiary of Sony Interactive Entertainment Inc. We plan, produce, and operate services centered around the 'Gran Turismo' series driving simulator, which has shipped over 100 million* cumulative copies worldwide.
*As of June 2025
https://www.polyphony.co.jp/
業務内容
「グランツーリスモ」に関わる外部企業様とのビジネス、コラボレーションを担当します。
・通訳、翻訳
・渉外、パートナーシップ
・制作＆開発のサポート
・英語での契約書作成、契約業務の経験
・プレゼンテーション資料の作成
グランツーリスモ・エクスプロアスタジオは、東京、福岡、ロサンゼルス、アムステルダムを拠点とし、それぞれにメンバーが在籍しています。世界各国の自動車メーカー、自動車関連企業、モータースポーツチームとのコミュニケーションを主務とし、世界各地のソニー・インタラクティブエンタテインメント各部門と協力しながら、ゲームへの自動車収録交渉、コラボレーション、プロモーション活動などにかかわる開発側の窓口を担当しています。また最近では、「ビジョン グランツーリスモ」プロジェクトに代表されるユニークなビジネス機会の創出と実現を担っています。
私たちは、未知の領域に踏み込んでいく好奇心や冒険心、前例の無いビジネスを生み出すクリエイティビティを持った仲間を求めています。
社内、社外を問わず、国籍や文化の異なる方々との日常のコミュニケーションは基本的に英語です。カンファレンスコール、メールコミュニケーション、ビジネスミーティングなどが主な業務で、そのほか世界各地で開催されるプロモーシナルイベント、モーターショー、モータースポーツイベントやゲームショーなどでは現場でのコミュニケーション業務を行います。
必要な条件・経験
・様々な国籍や文化をもつ人々とフレンドリーな関係を築ける
・メール、電話など英語でのビジネスコミュニケーションが行える
・希望する勤務地において、法的に就労できる資格（就労ビザなど）をお持ちである方
・英語含む外国語でのコミュニケーションスキル
・勤務地として東京・福岡を希望される場合は、ビジネスレベルの日本語力が必要です

Game Design Intern-Undergraduate
Location(s):
United States, Burbank, CA
Job ID:
R-125858
Apply Now
Share
Job ID: R-125858
Role Summary:
Join Insomniac Games as a Game Design Intern and gain hands-on experience creating and implementing gameplay content that helps bring game worlds to life.
As a Game Design Intern, you'll work alongside experienced designers to develop gameplay experiences, including world authoring, combat setups, event triggering and scripted structural elements. You'll apply your technical and creative skills while learning how game design ideas are developed and implemented throughout production.
Throughout the internship, you'll receive mentorship from senior team members, strengthen your design and problem-solving skills, and gain practical experience with game development tools and workflows. You'll also have opportunities to learn about the development process and how design teams create engaging narrative action games.
What You’ll Be Doing:
Create and implement gameplay content, including world authoring, combat setups, event triggering and scripted structural elements.
Use development tools such as Unity, Unreal Engine, Maya, or other 3D modeling software to support game design and content creation.
Apply visual scripting or programming skills to implement and refine gameplay features.
Collaborate with experienced designers to develop creative solutions and contribute to game development projects.
Explore how level design, gameplay mechanics, and scripted events work together to create engaging player experiences.
Learn and apply game design techniques and development workflows while receiving guidance and mentorship from senior team members.
What We’re Looking For:
Currently pursuing a Game Design degree at a college or university. Master's candidates may also apply.
Six to twelve months of related experience and/or training is beneficial but not required for currently enrolled students.
Basic familiarity with a 3D package such as Maya or 3DSMax is required; experience with Maya is preferred.
Scripting experience is required, preferably visual scripting, although knowledge of programming or scripting languages is also acceptable.
Familiarity with development tools such as Unity, Unreal Engine, Maya, or other 3D modeling software.
Strong technical skills, including visual scripting or programming experience.
Strong attention to detail, creativity, and an understanding of game design fundamentals.
A self-motivated and responsible approach to learning, with the ability to articulate creative goals and turn ideas into actionable solutions.
Passion for next-gen video games, with knowledge of the gaming industry and narrative action games.
Previous experience with level-editing tools, such as Unreal, Unity, or Hammer is a plus.
Eligibility:
Students with expected graduation between May 2026 and December 2029 from an accredited university. Undergraduate
Able to work during one of the following periods: May 17th - August 6th or June 14th - September 3rd. Relocation assistance is available for eligible candidates.
Must be available for the entire 12-week internship period.
At this time, SIE will only consider applicants who are authorized to work for any employer in the United States and who will not require work visa sponsorship from SIE now or in the future to maintain their authorization to work in the United States.
Our intern positions offer a fixed hourly pay rate. Please note that the pay rate is exclusive to the internship period and may not reflect what pay may look like following the completion of an internship.
At SIE, we consider several factors when setting each role’s base pay range, including the competitive benchmarking data for the market and geographic location.
Please note that the base pay range may vary in line with our hybrid working policy and individual base pay will be determined based on job-related factors which may include knowledge, skills, experience, and location.
Intern Pay Range
$36.50—$36.50 USD
Please note, Sony Interactive Entertainment conducts background checks at the offer stage for all new employees (which may include criminal background checks for some roles) and will need to process personal information to support these checks.
Please refer to our Candidate Privacy Notice for more information about what personal information we collect, how we use it, who we share it with, and your data protection rights.

Game Designer, Level (Contingent)
Location(s):
United States, Santa Monica, CA
Job ID:
R-124816
Apply Now
Share
This position is for temporary engagement through our staffing partner, Yoh Enterprise Solutions. Candidates must be authorized to work in the United States for this position.
This is a hybrid / onsite position based at our studio in Santa Monica, CA
For nearly 40 years, Naughty Dog has been crafting best-selling, critically acclaimed games that constantly push the boundaries of narrative, gameplay, and technology. Naughty Dog recently released The Last of Us Part II Remastered as well as a current generation remake of The Last of Us Part I for the PS5. We're also proud to say that The Last of Us Part I is now available for the first time on PC.
Naughty Dog games have long been lauded for their evocative, character-driven stories and attention to detail. We are committed to innovation within our games and as such have curated some of the top creative and technically innovative minds in the industry. We welcome diverse candidates and believe that a broad range of cultural and life experiences contribute to the overall well-being and success of the studio and the titles we craft together.
What you will do:
Design the layouts for your levels, blocking out simplified geometry to create engaging environments
Develop level composition, architectural concepts, and create evocative shapes in collaboration with the art department
Create scenarios, place game objects, and implement level progression using game editor tools
Produce and maintain documentation that establishes the core concepts of your level
Drive communication and collaboration for spaces you design as well as other parts of the game, ensuring communication across disciplines and meeting deadlines
Effective and respectful communicator and collaborator, both with other designers and members of other departments
Coordinate extensive playtesting, reviews, and iteration
Additional Responsibilities may be assigned as needed
What skills you will use:
3+ years of industry experience in Level Design experience for games
Experience blocking out environments for games using 3D software packages such as Maya, Unreal Editor or otherwise, that exemplify understanding of level design fundamentals
Experience using a game editor such as Unreal Editor and scripting/programming using a language such as C#, Lua, Python, Visual Scripting
Excitement for learning and growing as a designer. Keen to develop new skills.
Proficient and effective communication skills
Welcomes criticism and has experience collaborating as part of a team, innovating without losing sight of the gameplay vision
Understanding the single player gaming landscape
Maintains knowledge of the broader gaming landscape trends
Problem-solving skills and a proactive attitude.
As a part of the Naughty Dog application process, candidates will be expected to provide a portfolio or demo reel of their work and may be asked to complete a design test.
At SIE, working with our partners, we consider several factors when setting each role’s base pay range, including the competitive benchmarking data for the market and geographic location.
Please note that the individual base pay range may vary based on job-related factors which may include knowledge, skills, experience, and location.
In addition, this role is eligible for benefit offerings that include medical, dental, and vision. Click here to learn more.
The estimated base pay range for this role is listed below, this is an hourly rate.
$58.03—$65 USD
Please note, Sony Interactive Entertainment conducts background checks at the offer stage for all new employees (which may include criminal background checks for some roles) and will need to process personal information to support these checks.
Please refer to our Candidate Privacy Notice for more information about what personal information we collect, how we use it, who we share it with, and your data protection rights.

This position is for temporary engagement through our staffing partner, Yoh Enterprise Solutions. Candidates must be authorized to work in the United States for this position.
This is a hybrid / onsite position.
For nearly 40 years, Naughty Dog has been crafting best-selling, critically acclaimed games that constantly push the boundaries of narrative, gameplay, and technology. Naughty Dog recently released The Last of Us Part II Remastered as well as a current generation remake of The Last of Us Part I for the PS5 . We're also proud to say that The Last of Us Part I is now available for the first time on PC.
Naughty Dog games have long been lauded for their evocative, character-driven stories and attention to detail. We are committed to innovation within our games and as such have curated some of the top creative and technically innovative minds in the industry. We welcome diverse candidates and believe that a broad range of cultural and life experiences contribute to the overall well-being and success of the studio and the titles we craft together.
What you will do:
Prototyping, implementing, and owning gameplay scenarios, narrative‬ sequences, interactive objects, AI behaviors, and other mechanics and‬ systems‬.
Communicating with animation, programming, and art departments to‬ effectively produce the assets and code support required for the areas‬ you’re owning‬.
Collaborating closely with lead designers and directors to effectively act‬ on feedback and quickly iterate‬.
What skills will you use:
2+ years of industry experience in Technical Design for games‬
Strong programming ability (Computer Science undergraduate degree‬
‭ preferred)‬
Understanding of narrative principles and how to convey emotion‬ through gameplay‬
Proficiency in 3D math‬
Fluent in C++, C#, Lua, or other text-based scripting language‬
Self-starter with excellent communication and teamwork skills‬
‭
Bonus Skills‬
‬‭Experience developing third-person action games‬
Experience making improvements to tools and workflows‬
‬‭Experience with the following areas of design‬
Behavior Trees‬
Melee‬
Combat Systems‬
Enemy & boss design‬
‭
‭Sony is an Equal Opportunity Employer. All persons will receive consideration for employment without regard to gender (including gender identity, gender expression, and gender reassignment), race (including color, nationality, ethnic, national origin), religion or belief, marital or civil partnership status, disability, age, sexual orientation, pregnancy or maternity, trade union membership or membership in any other legally protected category.
We strive to create an inclusive environment, empower employees, and embrace diversity. We encourage everyone to respond.
At SIE, working with our partners, we consider several factors when setting each role’s base pay range, including the competitive benchmarking data for the market and geographic location.
Please note that the individual base pay range may vary based on job-related factors which may include knowledge, skills, experience, and location.
In addition, this role is eligible for benefit offerings that include medical, dental, and vision. Click here to learn more.
The estimated base pay range for this role is listed below, this is an hourly rate.
$56.70—$65 USD

Game Engineering Director
Location(s):
United States, Remote
Job ID:
R-121581
Apply Now
Share
Would you like to work on a new IP and new genre at teamLFG that combines amazing action feel with lighthearted and whimsical characters? Are you on a mission to create games that bond players together into deeply invested communities?
As an Engineering Director at teamLFG, you will hire, build and direct a technology organization to deliver amazing never-seen-before game experiences. Your organization will build and utilize a wide range of technologies and workflows to develop, continuously deliver, and maintain a live production game service. As part of the project and studio leadership team, you will create and evolve a technology strategy that is aligned with project goals and initiatives and serves the needs of the game, its players, and employees. As part of a diverse company that prizes creativity and technical excellence, your servant leadership will inspire and motivate a cross-discipline team to help build a new franchise beloved by millions.
We look for candidates who value joining a mission-focused team with a diverse set of backgrounds, experiences, interests, and viewpoints.
RESPONSIBILITIES
Collaborate with a cross-disciplinary project leadership team to develop and maintain a product technology strategy that is informed by product vision, technical costs, and long-term technology goals
Leveraging the Unreal Engine and technology the team has built, direct the engineering organization to tackle challenging problems, find combinations of innovative technology with proven models, and build reliable solutions that deliver game experiences while sensibly managing technology risks
Recruit, nurture, and retain an engineering organization (30+ engineers, both internal FTE and external codev) with the skills, capacity, and adaptability to navigate uncertainty and deliver on the changing needs of the project
Manage multiple senior engineering leads, providing management support, technical guidance, constructive feedback, and career development coaching
Work with production leadership to schedule, track, deliver, and close out game features and releases, anticipating and mitigating any critical risks encountered, technical or otherwise
Communicate with PlayStation executives, directors, and technology teams, as needed
REQUIRED SKILLS
Experience managing an organization of 20+ engineers on AAA game engineering teams
Experience working with and directing co-development engineering teams
Experience with developing and running online games in a live service environment
Ability to align with leadership and executive stakeholders, translate goals into strategic technology solutions with a thorough understanding of cost, efficiency, and quality tradeoffs, and then lead an organization to execute on a technology strategy
Effectively communicate with and inspire fellow engineers by driving team alignment and buy-in on technology vision and architectural principles
Ability to work with a team in a hybrid environment, leveraging real time chat communications and documentation across an entire game studio
Ability to communicate with stakeholders of different business, technical, and non-technical backgrounds, to help align objectives and negotiate common goals
Bachelors degree or equivalent
NICE TO HAVE SKILLS
Experience with development using the Unreal Engine
Experience with development on a variety of platforms (PC, console)
Experience with agile development methodologies
At SIE, we consider several factors when setting each role’s base pay range, including the competitive benchmarking data for the market and geographic location.
Please note that the base pay range may vary in line with our hybrid working policy and individual base pay will be determined based on job-related factors which may include knowledge, skills, experience, and location.
In addition, this role is eligible for SIE’s top-tier benefits package that includes medical, dental, vision, matching 401(k), paid time off, wellness program and coveted employee discounts for Sony products. This role also may be eligible for a bonus package. Click here to learn more.
This is a flexible role that can be remote, with varying pay ranges based on geographic location. For example, if you are based out of Seattle, the estimated base pay range for this role is listed below.
$255,000—$383,000 USD
Please note, Sony Interactive Entertainment conducts background checks at the offer stage for all new employees (which may include criminal background checks for some roles) and will need to process personal information to support these checks.
Please refer to our Candidate Privacy Notice for more information about what personal information we collect, how we use it, who we share it with, and your data protection rights.

3Dアーティスト・3D Artist/Team ASOBI
Location(s):
Japan, Tokyo
Job ID:
R-124964
Apply Now
Share
Why PlayStation?
PlayStation isn’t just the Best Place to Play — it’s also the Best Place to Work. Today, we’re recognized as a global leader in entertainment producing The PlayStation family of products and services including PlayStation®5, PlayStation®4, PlayStation®VR, PlayStation®Plus, acclaimed PlayStation software titles from PlayStation Studios, and more.
PlayStation also strives to create an inclusive environment that empowers employees and embraces diversity. We welcome and encourage everyone who has a passion and curiosity for innovation, technology, and play to explore our open positions and join our growing global team.
The PlayStation brand falls under Sony Interactive Entertainment, a wholly-owned subsidiary of Sony Group Corporation.
役職名:
3Dアーティスト
経験レベル:
経験レベル問わず応募可。
ゲーム制作の実務経験の有無は不問。
雇用形態:
正社員または契約社員。
フルタイム (自律性を保てるよう、ある程度の柔軟性を備えた1日8時間)
上長:
リードアーティスト
Studio及び部署:
PlayStation Studios Team ASOBI / アートチーム
勤務地:
東京エリア（オンサイト勤務）
役割概要:
Team ASOBIでは、情熱と才能を持つ新進気鋭の3Dアーティストを募集しています。このポジションでは、当社のゲームに登場する高品質なキャラクターやプロップ、背景などの制作を担当していただきます。必要に応じて、デザインに対する考え方や文化的な視点、制作技術に関するトレーニングを受けていただきます。 前向きに学ぶ姿勢と高い意欲を持ち、責任感をもって仕事に取り組める方を歓迎します。
役割・職責：
ゲームに必要な3Dモデルやテクスチャ、小物、背景など、各種アートアセットを制作します
より良いユーザー体験を実現するために、ゲーム開発に関わる他職種のメンバーと連携しながら制作を行います。
業務を通じて、ゲーム開発全般に関わるさまざまなスキルの習得が要求されます。柔軟に学び、吸収する姿勢が重要です。
必要スキル
アート、ゲームデザイン、ゲームそのものに対する強い情熱を持っていること。
英語の学習意欲のある方を歓迎（英会話レッスンを受講可能）
Maya、ZBrush、または同等のソフトウェアを用いたモデリングの実務レベルのスキル
Substance PainterおよびSubstance Designerを用いたテクスチャ制作スキル
カートゥーン調の遊び心あるアートスタイルに強い関心を持っていること。
スピード感を持って、自主的に業務へ取り組めること。
細部まで丁寧に作り込めること。
周囲への敬意を持ち、根気強く学ぶ姿勢を備えていること。
社交的で周囲と前向きに関わることができること。
注記： 選考の一環として、ご自身の時間および環境を用いた実技試験を受けていただきます。これに対応できない場合は、応募をご遠慮ください。
Job title:
3D Artist
Experience Level:
All levels. No past game experience required.
Employment type:
Permanent or Contract-based.
Full-time (8 hours per day with some flexibility for time).
Reporting to:
Lead Artist
Studio & Department:
PlayStation Studios Team ASOBI / Art Team
Location:
Tokyo area (expected to work onsite).
Role overview:
Team ASOBI is looking for a passionate and talented upcoming 3D Artist. The ideal candidate will be responsible for creating high quality characters, props and environment for our games. Where needed, will provide training and coaching to teach design philosophies, cultural thinking and techniques. We expect a positive learning attitude, high motivation and great commitment.
Responsibilities:
You will mostly create 3D models, textures, props, environments and other artistic elements needed for the game.
You will also collaborate with other discipline members involved in the creation of a game to achieve the best user experience.
Along the way, you will be required to learn many new general skills related to the development of a video game. An open mind will be crucial.
Skills required
Passion for Art, game design, and gaming.
Intermediate Japanese language skill.
Good modeling in Maya / Zbrush or other equivalent software.
Good texturing skills using substance painter and designer.
Strong interest in cartoony, playful art style.
Ability to work fast and autonomously.
Attention to detail.
Willingness and patience to learn with a respectful attitude.
Social and outgoing personality.
Note:
You will be required to pass a practical test using your own time and resources. Please refrain from applying if you cannot meet these criteria.
Equal Opportunity Statement:
Sony is an Equal Opportunity Employer. All persons will receive consideration for employment without regard to gender (including gender identity, gender expression and gender reassignment), race (including colour, nationality, ethnic or national origin), religion or belief, marital or civil partnership status, disability, age, sexual orientation, pregnancy or maternity, trade union membership or membership in any other legally protected category.
We strive to create an inclusive environment, empower employees and embrace diversity. We encourage everyone to respond.
PlayStation is a Fair Chance employer and qualified applicants with arrest and conviction records will be considered for employment.

Location(s):

Why PlayStation?
PlayStation isn’t just the Best Place to Play — it’s also the Best Place to Work. Today, we’re recognized as a global leader in entertainment producing The PlayStation family of products and services including PlayStation®5, PlayStation®4, PlayStation®VR, PlayStation®Plus, acclaimed PlayStation software titles from PlayStation Studios, and more.
PlayStation also strives to create an inclusive environment that empowers employees and embraces diversity. We welcome and encourage everyone who has a passion and curiosity for innovation, technology, and play to explore our open positions and join our growing global team.
The PlayStation brand falls under Sony Interactive Entertainment, a wholly-owned subsidiary of Sony Group Corporation.
経験レベル
全レベル。 ゲーム、長編映画、またはVFXでの実務経験必須。
雇用形態
正社員または契約社員
フルタイム (1日8時間)
上司
リード3Dエンバイロメントアーティスト
部署
PlayStation Studios Team ASOBI / アートチーム
勤務場所
東京エリア（６ヶ月の試用期間後、リモートワーク可。）
About Team ASOBI
Team ASOBIは、東京に拠点を置くPlayStationのファーストパーティスタジオで、PS5向けの大ヒット作『ASTRO BOT』を手掛けたチームです。遊び心にあふれたスタジオで、情熱を持った優れた才能たちが、協力しながら素晴らしい作品を生み出しています。今後も世界中から新たな才能を迎え入れ、さらなる成長を目指しています。Team ASOBIの使命は、すべてのプレイヤーが楽しめる、高品質で革新的なゲーム体験をPlayStationで提供することです。
▪️詳しい情報はPlayStation Blogをご覧ください！ https://blog.ja.playstation.com/2024/09/05/20240905-astrobot/
Role overview
Team ASOBI では、才能と情熱のある3Dエンバイロメントアーティストを募集しています。
エンバイロメントアーティストは、私達のゲームに遊び心のある優れた環境アートを作成することが求められます。
Responsibility
有機的かつ建築的で高品質の３Dモデルとテクスチャを作成すること
社内エディターを使用してレベルをレイアウトすること
レベルのトラブルシューティングを行い、デザインの整合性、視覚的品質、及びパフォーマンスを維持すること
エンバイロメントアートチームとレベルデザイナーと緊密に協力すること
求める経験とスキル
ゲーム開発、長編映画、またはVFXのエンバイロメントアーティストとしての経験があること
Maya / ZBrushもしくは同等のソフトウェアでの優れたモデリングとUVスキルにより、高品質な環境アセットを迅速に作成できること
意図したゲームプレイを向上させる美しい環境構成を作成する強力なスキル
最小限のコンセプトアートとディレクションでレベル全体を構想する強力なスキル
Substance Painter / Designerを使用した強力なテクスチャリングスキル
遊び心のあるアートスタイルに合ったリアルなテクスチャを扱うスキル
特定のアートスタイルに厳密に適応できること
自律的にタスクに取り組み、期日通りに結果を出せること
前向きな姿勢と優れたコミュニケーションスキルを備えたチームプレイ
タスク主導で、意欲が高く、積極的であること
アート、ゲームデザイン、ゲームへの情熱があること
流暢な日本語力、および英語の学習意欲のある方を歓迎（英会話レッスンを受講可能）
注記： 選考の一環として、ご自身の時間および環境を用いた実技試験を受けていただきます。これに対応できない場合は、応募をご遠慮ください。
About Team ASOBI
Team ASOBI is a first-party PlayStation Studio located in Tokyo, Japan, responsible for the critically acclaimed ASTRO BOT for PS5. The team is looking to expand by bringing worldwide talents with high skills, passion, and a playful, collaborative mind. The Studio has a mission to create top quality, innovative games for players of all ages on PlayStation.
Want to learn more? Head over to the PlayStation Blog: PlayStation Blog .
Role overview
Team ASOBI is looking for a talented 3D Environment Artist.
The ideal candidate will be responsible for creating playful and stunning looking environment art for our game.
Responsibilities
You create high quality 3D models and textures both organic and architectural.
Layout Levels using our inhouse editors.
Troubleshoot Levels and maintain their design integrity, visual quality, and performance.
Close collaboration with the Environment Art Team and Level Designers.
Required Skill and Experience
An experience as an Environment Artist in Game Development, Feature Film or VFX.
Excellent modeling and UV skills in Maya / Zbrush or other equivalent software to create high quality environment assets at a fast pace.
Strong skill at creating beautiful environment compositions that elevate the intended gameplay.
Strong ability to envision entire levels with minimal concept art and direction.
Strong texturing skills using substance painter and designer.
An emphasis on realistic texturing techniques applied to a playful art style.
Being able to closely adapt a specific art style
Being used to work on tasks autonomously and to deliver results on time
Team player with positive attitude and great communication skills
Task driven, highly motivated and proactive.
Passion for Art, game design, and gaming
Language proficiency
Basic Japanese language skill is required alongside desire to improve (lessons will be provided and paid for).
Note:
You will be required to pass a practical test using your own time and resources. Please refrain from applying if you cannot meet these criteria.
「Team ASOBI」
https://www.teamasobi.com/jp/

Sony is an Equal Opportunity Employer. AI Software Engineer
Location(s):
Belgium, Zaventem
Job ID:
R-124265
Apply Now
Share
Why Sony Interactive Entertainment?
Sony Interactive Entertainment isn’t just the Best Place to Play — it’s also the Best Place to Work. Sony Interactive Entertainment (SIE) is the company behind the PlayStation brand. As a subsidiary of Sony Group Corporation, we’re part of a proud legacy of innovation and excellence. SIE is a dynamic technology company, delivering cutting-edge hardware and network services to more than 100 million people and an entertainment leader, home to some of the most beloved and recognizable intellectual properties (IP) in the world. Our role at SIE is to create and nurture the experiences under the PlayStation brand, a name synonymous with entertainment excellence and creativity.
Hybrid Role - 1 day a week in the office
Who we are
The Direct to Consumer (D2C) Data Science organisation comprises Data Science, Data Engineering and ML Engineering practices. D2C Data Science helps PlayStation grow and operate its digital business across commerce, subscriptions, payments, lifecycle experiences and player-facing services. We partner with product, engineering, finance, marketing and operations teams to turn experimentation, forecasting, AI and production-quality measurement into better player experiences.
Role overview
Within D2C Data Science, the D2C ML Engineering team is seeking a software engineer to help design, build, and support production AI platform capabilities that solve high-value business problems across the broader digital commerce ecosystem. This is not a model-training or predictive-platform ownership role; it is an engineering role focused on turning AI into dependable products, services, and automation.
The ideal candidate is a strong software engineer with backend experience and practical hands-on applied AI experience.
What you’ll be doing:
Create services, workflows, and reusable components for LLM-powered solutions.
Collaborate with stakeholders to understand use cases, identify if and how AI could be used, prototype solutions, measure outcomes, and help move proven capabilities into production.
Develop scalable APIs, microservices, and event-driven workflows in Python or Java, with attention to reliability, resilience, security, cost efficiency, and clean integration with existing services.
What we’re looking for:
Master’s degree in computer science, engineering or related with 4+ years of professional software engineering experience.
Hands-on experience building AI or generative AI features that connect model APIs to business workflows, data, documents, or internal services.
Strong software engineering skills in Python and/or Java, including API development, testing, debugging, asynchronous processing, and maintainable service design.
Experience with AWS or equivalent cloud services
Possesses exceptional communication skills, able to turn business requirements into technical tasks, collaborate across teams, and explain AI tradeoffs in clear, practical terms.
Preferred Skills:
Experience with AI orchestration patterns
Experience supporting a production system
Familiarity with Model Context Protocol
Experience with text, image, document, audio, or video models, including multimodal embeddings, OCR/document understanding, or content moderation workflows.
Please note, Sony Interactive Entertainment conducts background checks at the offer stage for all new employees (which may include criminal background checks for some roles) and will need to process personal information to support these checks.
Please refer to our Candidate Privacy Notice (where applicable) for more information about what personal information we collect, how we use it, who we share it with, and your data protection rights.
Equal Opportunity Statement:
Sony is an Equal Opportunity Employer. All persons will receive consideration for employment without regard to gender (including gender identity, gender expression and gender reassignment), race (including colour, nationality, ethnic or national origin), religion or belief, marital or civil partnership status, disability, age, sexual orientation, pregnancy, maternity or parental status, trade union membership or membership in any other legally protected category.
We strive to create an inclusive environment, empower employees and embrace diversity. We encourage everyone to respond.
Sony Interactive Entertainment is a Fair Chance employer and qualified applicants with arrest and conviction records will be considered for employment.

Accessibility Intern- Master's or PhD
