```text
Job ID: R-125857
Role Summary:
Join Insomniac Games and gain hands-on experience helping make video games more accessible and inclusive for players with disabilities.
As an Accessibility Intern, you'll work alongside accessibility, design, and user research teams to support the development and improvement of accessibility features across game projects. You'll contribute to accessibility design, documentation, inclusive research, and feature implementation while learning how player feedback helps shape more accessible gaming experiences.
Throughout the internship, you'll receive guidance from experienced team members, collaborate with cross-functional partners, and gain practical experience applying accessibility principles to game development. You'll also have opportunities to learn from players with disabilities, accessibility consultants, and industry experts while developing your research, design, and communication skills.
What You’ll Be Doing:
Participate in project discussions and review feature lists and development plans to help identify opportunities to improve accessibility throughout game development.
Support accessibility design efforts by organizing and documenting features, including naming conventions, navigation patterns, and implementation workflows.
Collaborate with UI/UX and design partners to explore accessibility concepts, provide wireframe feedback, and contribute research insights to feature discussions.
Review gameplay to identify potential accessibility issues, document observations, and share findings with development teams.
Assist with planning and conducting inclusive research involving gamers who have disclosed accessibility needs, including consultants, employees, and external participants.
Observe and assist with moderating consultant sessions and, when appropriate, interviews with playtest participants.
Collect and analyze feedback from surveys, telemetry, interviews, observations, and video to identify recurring themes, player behaviors, and accessibility challenges.
Develop familiarity with accessibility guidelines and standards, including PlayStation Accessibility Standards (PAS), and learn from accessibility community perspectives, consultants, and industry experts.
Research relevant accessibility news, gaming articles, and community resources to support team discussions and ongoing learning.
Prepare accessibility updates, documentation, presentations, and supporting materials to communicate findings and recommendations to development teams.
Collaborate with SIE research partners and cross-functional teams on expert reviews, group studies, and accessibility initiatives.
What We’re Looking For:
Recently completed or currently pursuing a bachelor’s or higher degree in accessibility, inclusive design, game design, interaction design, UX design, or a related field.
Interest in accessibility, inclusive design, user research, and creating better gaming experiences for players with disabilities.
Strong verbal and written communication skills, with the ability to document observations, summarize information, and present findings to small internal groups with guidance.
Strong organizational skills, attention to detail, and the ability to follow instructions, ask questions, and manage assigned tasks.
Foundational ability to work with percentages, rates, and basic descriptive data, including reading tables and charts.
Working knowledge of Microsoft Office or Google Workspace.
Curiosity, problem-solving skills, and a willingness to learn from feedback and collaborate with cross-functional teams.
Interest in learning about video game production processes and accessibility features in games.
Coursework, portfolio projects, volunteer work, or professional experience demonstrating an interest in accessible design, inclusive game experiences, accessibility standards, or designing for people with disabilities is preferred.
Experience using accessibility features in games is a plus.
Familiarity with survey tools such as Qualtrics or SurveyMonkey is a plus, with training provided as needed.
Interest in developing quantitative research skills is encouraged.
Eligibility
Students with expected graduation between May 2026 and December 2029 from an accredited university. Advanced Degree- Master's or PhD
Able to work during one of the following periods: May 17th - August 6th or June 14th - September 3rd. Relocation assistance is available for eligible candidates.
Must be available for the entire 12-week internship period.
At this time, SIE will only consider applicants who are authorized to work for any employer in the United States and who will not require work visa sponsorship from SIE now or in the future to maintain their authorization to work in the United States.
Our intern positions offer a fixed hourly pay rate. Please note that the pay rate is exclusive to the internship period and may not reflect what pay may look like following the completion of an internship.
At SIE, we consider several factors when setting each role’s base pay range, including the competitive benchmarking data for the market and geographic location.
Please note that the base pay range may vary in line with our hybrid working policy and individual base pay will be determined based on job-related factors which may include knowledge, skills, experience, and location.
Intern Pay Range
$40.25—$46.75 USD
Please note, Sony Interactive Entertainment conducts background checks at the offer stage for all new employees (which may include criminal background checks for some roles) and will need to process personal information to support these checks.
```

```text
Why Sony Interactive Entertainment?
Sony Interactive Entertainment isn’t just the Best Place to Play — it’s also the Best Place to Work. Sony Interactive Entertainment (SIE) is the company behind the PlayStation brand. As a subsidiary of Sony Group Corporation, we’re part of a proud legacy of innovation and excellence. SIE is a dynamic technology company, delivering cutting-edge hardware and network services to more than 100 million people and an entertainment leader, home to some of the most beloved and recognizable intellectual properties (IP) in the world. Our role at SIE is to create and nurture the experiences under the PlayStation brand, a name synonymous with entertainment excellence and creativity.
The Asia Finance G&O team supports the PlayStation business and SIE’s broader business operations through Finance Operations.
This position is responsible for Finance Operations across Asia, primarily covering Korea, Singapore, Taiwan, and Hong Kong. With a primary focus on P2P and O2C activities, you will work closely with relevant business functions and global teams to drive improvements in operational quality, efficiency, and governance.
Finance Operations is a critical area of Finance that directly impacts revenue, cash flow, tax compliance, financial risk, and accounting quality. The role requires a strong focus on accuracy, compliance, and governance, while also delivering effective operations that support the needs of the business.
The role offers a flexible working environment combining office-based and remote work, as well as opportunities to broaden your experience in a global environment through collaboration with colleagues across different regions. You will also have opportunities to expand your career over the long term through job rotations and other development opportunities within Finance.
Responsibilities:
Oversee P2P operations across Asia, supporting effective invoice and payment processes while maintaining appropriate accounting, compliance, and financial controls.
Oversee O2C operations across Asia, supporting effective billing, accounts receivable, collection, and credit management processes to ensure timely and accurate execution and effective management of receivables and credit risk.
Guide BPO-delivered O2C and P2P operations by providing process support, maintaining key process documentation, monitoring execution, and resolving operational or control issues.
Drive improvements in operational quality and efficiency by enhancing and standardizing business processes, leveraging data, and exploring opportunities for automation. You will also collaborate with overseas offices and relevant functions to connect operational challenges and insights from Asia and other regions to broader global improvement initiatives.
Take on a broad range of assignments based on your experience and capabilities, including opportunities to contribute to cross-functional projects and initiatives involving collaboration with other regions.
Skills and Qualifications:
Must-Have:
5+ years of relevant experience in accounting or finance operations, with strong knowledge of O2C/AR, P2P/AP processes, and Korean VAT.
Practical SAP experience in a finance environment.
Strong ownership and problem-solving skills, with the ability to investigate issues and drive them through to resolution.
Effective English communication skills, with the ability to work across business teams, BPO partners, and regional/global stakeholders.
Ability to communicate effectively with a wide range of internal and external stakeholders, both domestically and internationally, and work collaboratively to drive problem-solving.
Korean language proficiency is a must-have requirement for this role.
Nice-to-Have:
Experience overseeing outsourced finance operations or working with BPO/shared-service organizations.
Experience working in a regional or multinational finance environment, including intercompany or cross-border operations.
Experience participating in finance transformation, system implementation, or process improvement projects.
Experience in data analysis and reporting using Excel, BI tools, or similar applications.
Japanese language skills.
Please note, Sony Interactive Entertainment conducts background checks at the offer stage for all new employees (which may include criminal background checks for some roles) and will need to process personal information to support these checks.
Please refer to our Candidate Privacy Notice (where applicable) for more information about what personal information we collect, how we use it, who we share it with, and your data protection rights.
Equal Opportunity Statement:
Sony is an Equal Opportunity Employer. All persons will receive consideration for employment without regard to gender (including gender identity, gender expression and gender reassignment), race (including colour, nationality, ethnic or national origin), religion or belief, marital or civil partnership status, disability, age, sexual orientation, pregnancy, maternity or parental status, trade union membership or membership in any other legally protected category.
We strive to create an inclusive environment, empower employees and embrace diversity. We encourage everyone to respond.
Sony Interactive Entertainment is a Fair Chance employer and qualified applicants with arrest and conviction records will be considered for employment.
```

```text
Administrative Assistant / Coordinator
Location(s):
United States, Aliso Viejo, CA
Job ID:
R-125622
Apply Now
Share
Why Sony Interactive Entertainment?
Sony Interactive Entertainment isn’t just the Best Place to Play — it’s also the Best Place to Work. Sony Interactive Entertainment (SIE) is the company behind the PlayStation brand. As a subsidiary of Sony Group Corporation, we’re part of a proud legacy of innovation and excellence. SIE is a dynamic technology company, delivering cutting-edge hardware and network services to more than 100 million people and an entertainment leader, home to some of the most beloved and recognizable intellectual properties (IP) in the world. Our role at SIE is to create and nurture the experiences under the PlayStation brand, a name synonymous with entertainment excellence and creativity.
Administrative Coordinator – Contractor
Location: Aliso Viejo, CA
Hybrid, Contract role - 12 months
About the Role
We are seeking an experienced Administrative Coordinator to provide administrative and operational support to a global team while supporting the day-to-day operations of the Aliso Viejo office. This role supports team members and stakeholders across multiple regions and time zones while serving as a key onsite resource for the local office.
Working in partnership with the Sr. Manager overseeing the site, the Administrative Coordinator will support day-to-day onsite operations, serve as an onsite point of contact, and help identify, coordinate, and escalate office needs as appropriate.
This is a highly collaborative and hands-on role with responsibilities spanning global team coordination, onsite operations, meetings, workshops, events, and administrative support. The ideal candidate is an experienced administrative professional who is comfortable navigating a fast-paced, global environment, managing multiple priorities, and partnering effectively across teams and functions.
While this position will not be dedicated to supporting an individual executive, experience with executive-level support is highly valued. The Administrative Coordinator should be comfortable providing backup executive support when needed.
Success in this role requires strong organizational skills, sound judgment, discretion, and initiative. The individual should be able to work independently within established procedures, manage day-to-day responsibilities with limited instruction, and recognize when guidance, alignment, or approval is needed.
Onsite Requirement: This role requires the ability to commute to the Aliso Viejo office at least three days per week, with flexibility to work onsite up to five days per week based on business needs. Additional onsite presence may be required for workshops, events, leadership visits, and other activities.
Key Responsibilities
Provide administrative and operational support to a global team, coordinating meetings, workshops, team events, leadership visits, and other activities across regions and time zones.
Manage meeting and event logistics, including scheduling, meeting spaces, catering, visitors, materials, equipment, and other operational requirements.
Partner with Executive Assistants, Facilities, IT, Security, Workplace, and other cross-functional partners to coordinate team and onsite needs.
Provide department and backup executive support as needed, including:
Managing meetings and schedules and supporting effective communication and follow-through among stakeholders.
Providing backup Executive Assistant support, including calendar management and other time-sensitive administrative support.
Coordinating domestic and international travel and processing expense reports as assigned.
Prepare correspondence, presentations, meeting materials, documentation, and meeting notes as needed.
Manage and track administrative requests, action items, deliverables, and deadlines to ensure timely follow-through and completion.
Maintain and organize team information, documentation, and resources across SharePoint and other collaboration platforms.
Exercise sound judgment and appropriate initiative, handle sensitive information with discretion, and identify and recommend improvements to administrative processes and operational efficiency.
Required Qualifications
7+ years of administrative support experience, ideally including a combination of department-level and executive support responsibilities.
Experience supporting a fast-paced, global or geographically distributed team or organization with multiple stakeholders and changing priorities.
Strong organizational and time-management skills, with exceptional attention to detail, ownership, and follow-through, and the ability to manage multiple priorities and deadlines simultaneously with accuracy.
Sound judgment and the ability to work independently, take appropriate initiative, and recognize when guidance, approval, or escalation is needed.
Excellent written and verbal communication and interpersonal skills, with the ability to work effectively in a collaborative team environment.
Ability to build effective working relationships across functions, organizational levels, cultures, and geographic regions.
Ability to handle sensitive and confidential information with discretion and remain composed in time-sensitive situations.
Proficiency in Microsoft 365 applications, including Outlook, Teams, SharePoint, Excel, PowerPoint, and Word.
Bachelor’s degree or equivalent combination of education and relevant professional experience.
Please note, Sony Interactive Entertainment conducts background checks at the offer stage for all new employees (which may include criminal background checks for some roles) and will need to process personal information to support these checks.
Please refer 
```

```text
Animation Assistant
Location(s):
United States, Santa Monica, CA
Job ID:
R-126027
Apply Now
Share
This position is for temporary engagement through our staffing partner Yoh Enterprise Solutions. Candidates must be authorized to work in the United States for this position.
This is a hybrid / onsite position based at our studio in Santa Monica, CA
For nearly 40 years, Naughty Dog has been crafting best-selling, critically acclaimed games that constantly push the boundaries of narrative, gameplay, and technology. Naughty Dog recently released The Last of Us Part II Remastered as well as a current generation remake of The Last of Us Part I for the PS5. We're also proud to say that The Last of Us Part I and Part II are now available for the first time on PC. Currently, we’re busy at the kennel working on Intergalactic: The Heretic Prophet, and we'd love to find talented individuals to join us!
Naughty Dog games have long been lauded for their evocative, character-driven stories and attention to detail. We are committed to innovation within our games and as such have curated some of the top creative and technically innovative minds in the industry. We welcome diverse candidates and believe that a broad range of cultural and life experiences contribute to the overall well-being and success of the studio and the titles we craft together.
Are you ready to get your start in the animation production process of a cutting edge AAA title? The animation team behind The Last of Us Part II could use your assistance!
We are looking for an eager individual that will help assist our animation team with removing blocks from their path forward. The demands of such a complex project come with a lot of steps both big and small as part of the animation process. We need help removing as many steps as we can to help the team focus the on maintaining the highest visual quality.
What you will do:
Prepare scenes and assets for animation and audio teams
Work closely with animators to ensure standards of quality are met
Additional responsibilities may be assigned as needed
What skills you will use:
Ability to learn quickly and adapt to our pipeline
Some experience with Maya or equivalent 3D animation software
Organized and methodical
Proactive communicator
Ability to work quickly and efficiently
Trustworthy and dependable with a positive “can do” attitude
Experience working in a team environment
Production experience a plus
Must be able to provide a demo reel or examples of work (Online preferred), a detailed breakdown of your contributions to each project, and an accompanying resume
As part of the Naughty Dog application process, candidates may be asked to complete an art test.
#LI-AB1
At SIE, working with our partners, we consider several factors when setting each role’s base pay range, including the competitive benchmarking data for the market and geographic location.
Please note that the individual base pay range may vary based on job-related factors which may include knowledge, skills, experience, and location.
In addition, this role is eligible for benefit offerings that include medical, dental, and vision. Click here to learn more.
The estimated base pay range for this role is listed below, this is an hourly rate.
$37.16—$46.44 USD
```

```text
Animation Intern (Gameplay)- Undergraduate
Location(s):
United States, Burbank, CA
Job ID:
R-125851
Apply Now
Share
Job ID: R-125851
Role Summary:
Join the animation team at Insomniac Games and gain hands-on experience creating animations that bring characters and game worlds to life.
As an Animation Intern, you'll work alongside experienced animators and content creators to develop animations for in-game characters, props, and cinematics. You'll contribute to game production projects while learning the techniques and tools used to create engaging gameplay experiences.
This internship offers the opportunity to strengthen your animation skills, receive guidance from experienced team members, and gain practical experience in a collaborative game development studio.
What You’ll Be Doing:
Create a variety of animations for in-game characters, props, and cinematics that contribute to game development projects.
Support the animation team with assigned tasks, including animation development and bug fixing.
Learn and apply new animation techniques and tools to bring characters to life.
Collaborate with experienced animators and content creators across the studio to develop and refine animation work.
Gain hands-on experience with animation workflows and game production while strengthening your artistic, technical, and collaborative skills.
What We’re Looking For:
Currently pursuing a Bachelor's degree in Computer, Traditional, or Experimental Animation. Students majoring in Art, Illustration, or Graphic Design with animation experience will also be considered.
Six to twelve months of related experience and/or training is beneficial but not required for currently enrolled students.
A creative, self-motivated, and responsible approach to learning and completing assigned work.
Passion for video games and animation, with a willingness to learn new techniques and contribute to team projects.
Introductory 3D animation experience, drawing skills and previous experience with Maya are preferred.
Eligibility
Students with expected graduation between May 2026 and December 2029 from an accredited university. Undergraduate
Able to work during one of the following periods: May 17th - August 6th or June 14th - September 3rd. Relocation assistance is available for eligible candidates.
Must be available for the entire 12-week internship period.
At this time, SIE will only consider applicants who are authorized to work for any employer in the United States and who will not require work visa sponsorship from SIE now or in the future to maintain their authorization to work in the United States.
Our intern positions offer a fixed hourly pay rate. Please note that the pay rate is exclusive to the internship period and may not reflect what pay may look like following the completion of an internship.
At SIE, we consider several factors when setting each role’s base pay range, including the competitive benchmarking data for the market and geographic location.
Please note that the base pay range may vary in line with our hybrid working policy and individual base pay will be determined based on job-related factors which may include knowledge, skills, experience, and location.
Intern Pay Range
$36.50—$36.50 USD
Please note, Sony Interactive Entertainment conducts background checks at the offer stage for all new employees (which may include criminal background checks for some roles) and will need to process personal information to support these checks.
Please refer to our Candidate Privacy Notice for more information about what personal information we collect, how we use it, who we share it with, and your data protection rights.
```

```text
Why Sony Interactive Entertainment?
Sony Interactive Entertainment isn’t just the Best Place to Play — it’s also the Best Place to Work. Sony Interactive Entertainment (SIE) is the company behind the PlayStation brand. As a subsidiary of Sony Group Corporation, we’re part of a proud legacy of innovation and excellence. SIE is a dynamic technology company, delivering cutting-edge hardware and network services to more than 100 million people and an entertainment leader, home to some of the most beloved and recognizable intellectual properties (IP) in the world. Our role at SIE is to create and nurture the experiences under the PlayStation brand, a name synonymous with entertainment excellence and creativity.
This position is for temporary engagement through our staffing partner, Yoh Enterprise Solutions. This is a 12-month contract.
Animation Tools Programmer
Position Overview:
We have a great opportunity to join PlayStation Studios. We are seeking an Animation Tools Programmer to assist with development and improvement of our current suite of tools used for our MLB The Show product line. Your efforts will directly impact the next generation of The Show. If this is you, then you need to come play with us here in sunny San Diego.
Responsibilities:
Designing and implementing systems to support our animation systems.
Helping maintain and improve existing systems.
Writing clear, maintainable, portable, and highly functional code.
Profiling and performance tuning of code and systems to remove bottlenecks.
Work on tools and technology tasks to support animation project requirements.
Testing and debugging of your code and that of others past and present.
Collaborate with designers to turn ideas into concrete game features.
REQUIREMENTS:
B.S. degree in computer science or equivalent experience.
Minimum of 3 years programming experience.
Expertise in C++.
Self-motivated with good communication skills.
Able and motivated to work with other team members.
Commitment to code quality, and sound testing procedures.
Knowledge of baseball rules and strategies is a plus.
Sony is an Equal Opportunity Employer. All persons will receive consideration for employment without regard to race, color, religion, gender, pregnancy, national origin, ancestry, citizenship, age, legally protected physical or mental disability, covered veteran status, status in the U.S. uniformed services, sexual orientation, gender identity, marital status, genetic information or membership in any other legally protected category.
We strive to create an inclusive environment, empower employees and embrace diversity. We encourage everyone to respond.
We sincerely appreciate the time and effort you spent in contacting us, and we thank you for your interest in PlayStation.
At SIE, working with our partners, we consider several factors when setting each role’s base pay range, including the competitive benchmarking data for the market and geographic location.
Please note that the individual base pay range may vary based on job-related factors which may include knowledge, skills, experience, and location.
In addition, this role is eligible for benefit offerings that include medical, dental, and vision. Click here to learn more.
The estimated base pay range for this role is listed below, this is an hourly rate.
$47.98—$59.95 USD
Please note, Sony Interactive Entertainment conducts background checks at the offer stage for all new employees (which may include criminal background checks for some roles) and will need to process personal information to support these checks.
Please refer to our Candidate Privacy Notice (where applicable) for more information about what personal information we collect, how we use it, who we share it with, and your data protection rights.
Equal Opportunity Statement:
Sony is an Equal Opportunity Employer. All persons will receive consideration for employment without regard to gender (including gender identity, gender expression and gender reassignment), race (including colour, nationality, ethnic or national origin), religion or belief, marital or civil partnership status, disability, age, sexual orientation, pregnancy, maternity or parental status, trade union membership or membership in any other legally protected category.
We strive to create an inclusive environment, empower employees and embrace diversity. We encourage everyone to respond.
Sony Interactive Entertainment is a Fair Chance employer and qualified applicants with arrest and conviction records will be considered for employment.
```

```text
Associate Development Director
Location(s):
Finland, Helsinki
Job ID:
R-125478
Apply Now
Share
OUR STUDIO
At Housemarque, we believe gameplay always comes first. With technical excellence and compelling narrative, spiced with our trademark neon flair and top-notch audiovisual feedback, we want our players to always feel actively engaged with the worlds we have created for them.
Our award-winning game Returnal, released on PS5 in 2021, and Saros, released in 2026, embrace all these qualities. THIS is our DNA!
We value Nordic work-life balance by growing sustainably and ensuring our employees have the time and means to develop personally and professionally during work time.
We were established in 1995 with strong roots in the Finnish demoscene. In June 2021 we joined the PlayStation Studios, after years of partnership.
If you wish to join us for our next journey, apply today!
The Job
This is an opportunity to help shape the future of Housemarque, the award-winning PlayStation studio behind Returnal and SAROS.
We’re looking for an experienced development and project management leader to drive the planning, execution and continuous improvement of a large-scale console game project.
Working in close partnership with Product, Creative and Game leadership, you’ll lead the project management discipline across the project; bringing together scope, resources, budget, risk and development planning to create a clear path towards our goals.
This is a highly collaborative leadership role with significant influence across the project and studio. You’ll bring clarity to complex development challenges, strengthen how we plan and work at scale, and ensure our teams have the structures and processes they need to do their best work.
What you will do
Lead project management across a large-scale console game project, driving planning and execution against key milestones and deliverables.
Partner with Product, Creative and Game leadership to turn project goals and priorities into clear, achievable development plans.
Lead project-wide scope, capacity and resource planning, balancing ambition, available resources, timelines and budget.
Establish effective risk management practices, identifying high-impact risks early and driving solutions alongside project leadership.
Develop and continuously improve workflows, processes and organisational structures that enable teams to collaborate and deliver effectively at scale.
Own project-level budgeting and forecasting, and represent the project in broader studio resource and capacity planning.
Coordinate complex development dependencies across internal teams, external partners and co-development studios.
Provide clear visibility into project health, priorities, risks and trade-offs to studio leadership and key stakeholders.
Who you are
You have significant experience leading production, development management or project management for large-scale, AAA, Console / PC game development.
You have experience guiding complex game projects through multiple stages of development, ideally through to launch.
You bring strong project management expertise and are comfortable managing complex schedules, budgets, resources and dependencies across large multidisciplinary teams.
You understand how to manage scope effectively, balancing creative ambition with production realities and enabling informed decision-making.
You are confident in identifying and managing project risk, bringing structure and sound judgement to complex or ambiguous development challenges.
You are an excellent communicator who can build trust, influence senior stakeholders and create alignment across creative, technical, product and production disciplines.
You are comfortable operating at scale, including working with distributed teams, co-development partners, outsourcing or external vendors.
You have a continuous improvement mindset and know how to introduce structure and evolve processes without creating unnecessary overhead.
Bonus points
Experience coordinating development across multiple studios or international teams.
Experience establishing or significantly evolving production processes within a growing or changing studio.
Experience with portfolio-level capacity planning, resource management or production forecasting.
Please note, Sony Interactive Entertainment conducts background checks at the offer stage for all new employees (which may include criminal background checks for some roles) and will need to process personal information to support these checks.
Please refer to our Candidate Privacy Notice for more information about what personal information we collect, how we use it, who we share it with, and your data protection rights.
Equal Opportunity Statement
Housemarque is proud to be an Equal Opportunity Employer with a commitment to a culture of inclusion and mutual respect for all employees from every walk of life, reflecting the community we make games for. We welcome and encourage applications from all suitable candidates regardless of age, disability, gender, sexual orientation, pregnancy and maternity, race and ethnicity, religion or belief, and marriage and civil partnerships. We strive to create an inclusive environment, empower employees and embrace diversity.
```

```text
Associate Game Designer, Technical (Contingent)
Location(s):
United States, Santa Monica, CA
Job ID:
R-124656
Apply Now
Share
This position is for temporary engagement through our staffing partner, Yoh Enterprise Solutions. Candidates must be authorized to work in the United States for this position.
This is a hybrid / onsite position.
For nearly 40 years, Naughty Dog has been crafting best-selling, critically acclaimed games that constantly push the boundaries of narrative, gameplay, and technology. Naughty Dog recently released The Last of Us Part II Remastered as well as a current generation remake of The Last of Us Part I for the PS5. We're also proud to say that The Last of Us Part I is now available for the first time on PC.
Naughty Dog games have long been lauded for their evocative, character-driven stories and attention to detail. We are committed to innovation within our games and as such have curated some of the top creative and technically innovative minds in the industry. We welcome diverse candidates and believe that a broad range of cultural and life experiences contribute to the overall well-being and success of the studio and the titles we craft together.
In this role you’ll be responsible for prototyping gameplay using our in-house tools and scripting language, which could range from combat encounters to narrative sequences to player mechanics and enemy behaviors. Then working across departments to bring your prototypes into production and polish your work to the high level of quality Naughty Dog is known for. We’re looking for self-motivated, proactive designers who enjoy creating unique experiences and can combine their technical and creative skills while iterating rapidly.
What you will do
Prototyping, implementing, and owning gameplay scenarios, narrative sequences, interactive objects, and AI behaviors
Collaborating with the Design team to conceptualize, prototype, implement, tune, and balance gameplay mechanics and systems
Communicating with animation, programming, and art departments to effectively produce the assets and code support required for the areas you’re owning
Additional responsibilities may be assigned as needed
What skills will you use
Significant programming ability (Computer Science undergraduate degree preferred)
Knowledge of 3D math
Experience with C++, C#, Lua, or other scripting language
Broad understanding of gaming landscape
Self-starter with excellent communication and teamwork skills
Willingness to take design direction when offered, brainstorm, iterate and come up with fun and creative solutions
Additional responsibilities may be assigned as needed
Bonus Skills
Experience developing third-person action games
Experience with 3D level editors
Understanding of narrative principles, and conveying emotion through gameplay
Experience with the following areas of design
Behavior Trees
Melee
Combat Systems
Character animation
Enemy & boss design
As part of the Naughty Dog application process, candidates should expect to be given a technical interview
#LI-SP3

Please note that the individual base pay range may vary based on job-related factors which may include knowledge, skills, experience, and location.
In addition, this role is eligible for benefit offerings that include medical, dental, and vision. Click here to learn more.
The estimated base pay range for this role is listed below, this is an hourly rate.
$37—$50 USD
```
